However, current simulators produce overly cooperative clients that disclose too readily, accept therapeutic reframes without resistance, and resolve core issues within a single session. We trace these issues to profiles that lack causal depth and behavioral mechanisms that treat all content as equally accessible. We present PatientAct, a framework for client simulation grounded in established clinical theories. Our profiles integrate the 5Ps clinical case formulation, providing causal depth without tying the design to any single therapeutic modality. During simulation, profiles include a dynamic memory layer in which items carry trust thresholds (e.g., symptoms are available early, whereas formative memories require a sustained therapeutic alliance). At each turn, the client's emotional reaction and behavior are modeled before generating a response. If the therapist approaches gated content, PatientAct expresses resistance in terms of quantity, content, and style rather than defaulting to cooperation or a single resistance pattern. We evaluate our framework on 40 clinical situations and demonstrate that it generates diverse profiles with high clinical plausibility. Moreover, PatientAct significantly outperforms the baselines, yielding substantial gains in resistance quality and behavioral realism. Our code and data will be publicly available via github.com/Sahandfer/PatientHub.

</details>


### [36] [ReconSpan: Reconstruction-Guided Adaptive Latent Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12756)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReconSpan，一种自适应潜在标记化方法，通过将文本划分为可由单向（backward）解码器从单一上下文前缀代码重建的片段，并保留该代码作为潜在标记，从而在压缩与信息保留之间取得平衡。训练后的自动编码器可产生6.5到12.2的平均块长度，重建引导的边界比随机边界保留更多文本信息，但读者（解码器）虽能可靠恢复主题信息，却难以提取精确细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在标记化方法通常采用固定粒度或均匀切分，无法根据输入内容自适应地分配表示长度。作者希望探索输入相关的可变跨度分割，使较细粒度的输入被压缩为更短的连续表示序列，同时保证重建质量。

Method: ReconSpan将文本划分为若干片段（chunk），每个片段由一个backward解码器从单一上下文前缀代码重建，并保留该代码作为该片段的潜在标记。重建准则在片段形成时即被应用，使自动编码器能够自适应地生成不同平均块长度（6.5至12.2）。

Result: 在匹配的平均块长度下，重建引导的边界划分比随机边界保留了更多的文本信息。对潜在序列的阅读者（下游解码器）能够可靠地恢复主题信息，但在提取精确细节方面表现困难。

Conclusion: ReconSpan证明了基于重建准则的自适应潜在标记化是可行的，能够在压缩率与信息保留之间取得权衡，但揭示了潜在序列在粗粒度信息（主题）传递方面优于细粒度信息（细节）保留的局限性。

Abstract: Adaptive latent tokenization maps a fine-grained input to a shorter sequence of continuous representations associated with input-dependent spans. We introduce ReconSpan, which divides text into chunks that a backward decoder can reconstruct from a single contextual prefix code and retains one such code as the latent token for each chunk. The reconstruction criterion is applied when chunks are formed, allowing one trained autoencoder to produce average chunk lengths from 6.5 to 12.2. At matched average length, reconstruction-guided boundaries preserve more text than random boundaries. Readers of the resulting latent sequence recover topic information reliably but struggle to extract exact details.

</details>
