However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [74] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了O-RAN架构中基于机器学习的安全防御技术所引入的能量开销问题，指出在重视ML准确性和鲁棒性的同时，防御机制的能耗成本常被忽视，并对ML准确性、鲁棒性与能量效率三者之间的权衡挑战进行了刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络各层面广泛集成AI/ML算法（如O-RAN的RIC），提升了资源分配、负载均衡和能效，但数据依赖引入了新的安全漏洞。现有防御策略虽然有效，却带来了计算开销和相应的能量成本，而这一成本在当下强调能源意识的背景下仍被普遍忽视。

Method: 本文表征了防御技术的能耗特性，系统性地勾勒了ML准确性、鲁棒性和能量效率这一三元组合所面临的挑战。

Result: 量化刻画了防御机制造成的能量消耗，并明确了同时兼顾ML精度、模型鲁棒性和系统能效时存在的内在矛盾与权衡问题。

Conclusion: 在网络智能化与安全防御日益重要的背景下，未来的研究需在保证防御有效性的同时，将能量效率纳入设计考量，以实现ML准确性、鲁棒性与能效的均衡。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [75] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个结构性缺陷：基于无条件风险界限的自动化决策认证可能因"从不行动的选择器"而空洞失效。作者提出"决策契约"理论，包含误差守恒律、单例容量认证和非常规可行动性认证，并在ATT&CK对齐的LLM入侵检测告警分流中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无条件风险界限可通过从不行动的决策器（永不触发的选择器）使风险归零，导致认证空洞化。作者证明这是结构性的：任何风险证书都建立在"决策契约"（系统行为的输入加输出正确的语义关系）之上，弱化任一要素都会隐藏基类分类器误差。该问题在LLM入侵检测的告警分流场景中实际暴露，需要一种能排除"全弃权"方案的实质性认证框架。

Method: 作者发展了一套决策契约理论，包含三个核心组件：(1) 误差守恒律——证明误差仅在有害自动化、人类延迟决策和语义掩蔽三类间重新分配而非消除；(2) 无标签单例容量认证——用单个样本即可证明结构性无能，并配有风险可行细化，区分可恢复的阈值错位与受风险约束的无能；(3) 非常规可行动性认证——通过构造排除所有全弃权解。将该框架实例化到ATT&CK对齐告警分流场景，用于LLM入侵检测。

Result: 在3个IDS数据集、6种LLM和4个错误率阈值下：90.3%的配置中经验虚假归因风险保持在目标值或以下，83.4%的平均正确自动化率。容量诊断解释了所有低效用配置；其细化能力区分了真正的错位与受风险约束的无能，并通过展示替代阈值得到确认。训练稳定性复现未发现确认的结构性无能实例；真实细粒度攻击子类型标签在真正的多对一映射下确认了粗化转移恒等性，掩蔽质量虽小但非零。

Conclusion: 无条件风险界限认证必须建立在明确的决策契约之上，否则会因全弃权解而空洞失效。所提出的决策契约理论通过误差守恒律、单例容量认证和非常规可行动性认证，提供了排除退化解的实质性认证框架，并在LLM入侵检测告警分流中展现出良好的实用有效性。该工作将"可行动性"从隐含假设提升为必须显式构造的认证条件。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [76] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从软件工程视角对2010-2025年间290篇隐私文档研究论文进行了系统化综述（SoK），围绕隐私文档的定义与范围、生成、分析与提取、一致性与合规性检查、可用性评估与改进五个研究问题进行梳理，识别出15个关键研究趋势、21个开放机会，并指出AI中心平台挑战、多样化数据基础需求、基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性等四大研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档（如隐私政策）是数字服务披露数据实践和获取用户同意的核心机制，然而随着研究领域不断扩展（涵盖传统隐私政策、隐私标签、界面级透明度机制等），研究者越来越难以获得关于隐私文档在其生命周期中如何创建、分析、评估和维护的连贯视图。

Method: 采用SoK（Systematization of Knowledge）方法论，系统性地回顾和分析了2010年至2025年间发表的290篇论文，将其组织在五个研究问题框架下：（1）隐私文档的定义与范围、（2）生成、（3）分析与提取、（4）不一致性和不合规性检查、（5）可用性评估与改进。

Result: 基于对这290篇论文的系统分析，本文识别出15个关键研究趋势和21个开放研究机会，并进一步绘制了四个更广泛的研究方向：AI中心平台的新兴挑战、多样化且最新的数据基础需求、基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析，以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性。

Conclusion: 本文期望通过提供隐私文档生命周期视角的统一综述，为未来在隐私政策和隐私文档领域的研究建立共享基础。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [77] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种新型水印方法，同时嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号，实现LLM生成文本的来源追溯与篡改检测，支持完整、篡改、无水印三态判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法在编辑下保持来源追溯的鲁棒性，但这也使得攻击者可以修改关键内容而仍保留归属，即"piggyback spoofing"（搭便车欺骗）漏洞。需要一种既能追溯来源又能提供篡改证据的水印方法。

Method: 在每个生成的token中共同嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号。两种信号共享相同的机制，但使用独立的密钥和不同的种子窗口（基于归一化文本），使一种对编辑鲁棒、另一种对读者可见的变化敏感。采用多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保持期望生成分布，通过周期性的轮次分配模式控制两种信号间的权衡。检测时，两个信号的分数构成二维空间，支持完整（Intact）、篡改（Tampered）和无水印（No-Watermark）三种决策。

Result: 在两个大语言模型和两个提示数据集上的实验表明，该方法在篡改检测率上优于被评估的其他方法，同时保持竞争力的归属鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究证实，可靠的三态检测需要明确定义的完整性概念、两种信号的共同嵌入，以及互补的编辑敏感性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过共同嵌入鲁棒与脆弱两种信号，在保持来源追溯能力的同时实现了对文本篡改的有效检测，为LLM生成文本的完整性和真实性验证提供了一种新的三态判定机制。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [78] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIPES防御框架，利用语义先验和来源溯源信息对工具使用型智能体的响应单元进行筛查，从而防御状态腐败攻击（state-corruption attacks）。在VitaBench和AgentDyn基准上，PIPES将攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持（甚至略提升）良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体从不同信任级别的外部来源消费数据，但工具响应通常不标明每个组件的生产者或用途。这一缺口使得攻击者可通过受控内容进行状态腐败攻击——即让响应组件超出其信息权威范围做出环境断言，从而污染智能体的感知环境，使后续动作在现有护栏看来是"合理"的。

Method: PIPES（Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening）通过两步筛查响应单元：(1) 当schema提供稳定预期时，使用静态字段契约（static field contracts）进行筛选；(2) 对于开放式内容，基于响应前轨迹（pre-response trajectory）和可信溯源元数据（provenance metadata）对其筛查。系统标记违反语义先验或溯源层次结构的单元，部署方可根据需要选择移除、警告、阻止或升级处理。论文在原子移除模式下，针对自适应PAIR风格攻击进行评估。

Result: 在VitaBench的三个split和AgentDyn的三个split上，以Gemma 4 31B IT为目标智能体，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%；良性效用从90.6%提升至92.5%，说明防御几乎不牺牲（甚至略微提升）正常场景下的性能。

Conclusion: 溯源感知的筛查机制能有效防御工具使用智能体的状态腐败攻击，且不损害良性效用；该方法为工具不可信输入环境下的智能体安全提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [79] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealmEye 是首个面向 Arm CCA Realm VM 的虚拟机自省（VMI）系统，将全部自省逻辑放置在 Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的 R-EL2 层级，实现了无需 in-VM agent 的硬件强制隔离，支持内存/寄存器读取、一致性快照和页级访问捕获，并通过硬件认证通道向远程所有者返回结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（CVM）的硬件隔离在保护虚拟机免受恶意云厂商攻击的同时，也使得虚拟机所有者无法感知内部运行状态：内核 rootkit 可以通过网络或供应链攻击植入，在隔离掩护下隐藏进程、篡改内核数据或窃取模型权重。经典的 VM introspection（VMI）假设可信 Hypervisor 存在，但 CVM 将 Hypervisor 排除在 TCB 之外。现有的最佳方案 00SEVen 依赖 AMD SEV-SNP 特有的 VMPL0 权限层级，在 Arm CCA 架构上不存在该机制，导致 Realm VM 缺乏自省解决方案。

Method: RealmEye将完整的自省逻辑部署在 Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的 R-EL2 特权层级，实现监控器与被监控 Realm 之间的硬件强制分离。核心设计包括：(1) 直接读取 Realm 内存与寄存器；(2) 暂停 VM 以获取一致性快照；(3) 捕获页级访问事件；(4) 周期性自驱动触发模式使扫描时序内置于 RMM，防止 Hypervisor 与 in-Realm rootkit 串谋；(5) 通过硬件认证通道将结果返回远程所有者；(6) 提供 CCA 驱动后端，使 LibVMI 和 DRAKVUF 等现有工具无需修改即可互操作。

Result: 在 Arm Fixed Virtual Platform（FVP）上进行评估，RealmEye 成功检测到 Diamorphine rootkit 的进程隐藏和 syscall-table hooking；其 in-RMM 开销可由原始调用计数线性预测。

Conclusion: RealmEye 填补了 Arm CCA 架构上 CVM 自省的空白，证明了在 RMM 层级构建通用、可扩展且与现有生态兼容的 VMI 系统的可行性，为 Arm 机密计算环境提供了首个实用的运行时完整性监控方案。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码作为安全工件进行了系统性实证研究，发现PyPI生态中存在大量字节码制品（7,388个包、228,578个.pyc文件），并揭示了字节码分析的可操作性与CPython运行时在对抗性字节码下的鲁棒性问题，包括17种不同的鲁棒性签名和1,009个运行时发现，且无法从源代码层面复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全分析主要聚焦于源代码层面，但Python运行时可直接执行字节码（.pyc文件、编译模块、marshalled code对象），导致"检查-执行"之间存在鸿沟，即静态审查看到的内容与实际执行的内容不一致，构成潜在的安全盲区。

Method: 对1,034,843个PyPI制品进行字节码暴露测量；使用版本感知的反编译器评估字节码可分析性；通过对抗性字节码对CPython运行时（3.8-3.14）进行鲁棒性测试，包括模糊测试和变异字节码；最后检验源代码层面是否能复现字节码发现。

Result: 识别出7,388个含字节码的工件和228,578个.pyc文件；204,901/204,904个现代CPython字节码文件至少被一种反编译器成功输出源码；观察到的PyPI字节码能触发托管代码异常和超时，变异字节码可导致反编译器本地进程崩溃，共产生17种鲁棒性签名；模糊测试产生1,009个运行时发现，261组具有潜在内存损坏特征；至少91.7%的发现组可执行超过文档标注的不安全摄入边界；所有发现均无法从普通Python源代码复现。

Conclusion: 字节码是PyPI生态中可见、可分析且具有安全相关性的解释器输入，其行为不一定与源码层面的行为一致，因此安全分析必须将字节码视为独立的分析对象而非假定其与源码等价。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [81] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可扩展、与应用无关的框架，用于从大规模区块链活动中发现持久行为模式。框架构建行为句子并结合合约、代币和市场上下文，通过两层嵌入（句子级和序列级）捕捉个体行为与用户随时间的行为序列，并利用可解释的行为画像器对社区进行刻画。在以太坊超过三千万笔交易上的评估显示，该框架能识别常规与恶意行为模式（如DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和rug-pull骗局），且多数模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为识别与区块链取证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链DeFi分析多为应用特定、难以扩展或难以解释，缺乏一个通用、可扩展且可解释的框架来发现大规模区块链活动中持久的行为模式。

Method: 构建富含合约、代币和市场上下文的行为句子，采用两步嵌入流程：句子级嵌入捕获个体动作，序列级嵌入捕获用户随时间的行为；通过可解释的行为画像器利用行为基元、常规模式、时间动态、实体暴露和可疑性证据来刻画发现的社区。

Result: 在以太坊3000多万笔交易上的评估表明，该框架能同时揭示常规和恶意行为模式，包括DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和rug-pull方案。许多模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为识别。

Conclusion: 该框架结合了可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析，可用于区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [82] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对工具型AI代理的安全评估进行了审计，发现历史评估标注直接受处理方案元数据泄露影响（即相同的固定行为在处理方案重新标注下会改变类别），通过处理方案盲化重建修正了58条错误的ATTACK_SUCCESS/HIJACK_ATTEMPT标签，同时保留3条验证过的受保护数据传输和1条独立转发案例。锁定版v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，双审者盲法一致性复核在4个构造边界案例上与锁定代码本存在分歧。研究贡献了七环节完整性链和可执行的端点完整性linter，并明确了该结果是活动边界内的测量审计，而非人口攻击率、模型排名、防御效力或因果估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具型AI代理安全评估常将存储的标签等同于行为事实，但本文通过审计说明历史标签可能受处理方案元数据泄露污染，挑战了评估标签的可信度，旨在揭示评估流程中的系统性偏差。

Method: 对保留的活动进行审计：追踪10,200条执行记录至180个模型绑定请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激；比较两种处理方案与外部载荷族语料库（未交付）；进行处理方案盲化重建、锁定版v2普查、双审者盲法一致性复核，并构建七环节完整性链与端点完整性linter。

Result: 发现历史评分器存在直接处理泄露：处理元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类别；盲化重建修正58条标签为授权良性完成，保留3条验证的受保护数据传输和1条转发案例；v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS为零，转发案例仍为HIJACK_ATTEMPT；双审者一致性复核与锁定代码本在4个构造边界案例上不一致。

Conclusion: 该研究的贡献是一个活动限定的测量审计框架（七环节完整性链+端点完整性linter），而非攻击率、模型排名、防御效力或因果关系的估计；强调安全评估必须将标签与可追溯的行为事实分离，并防止处理元数据污染标签。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [83] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种模型（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）在垃圾短信（Smishing）检测中面对字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动三种对抗攻击时的鲁棒性，发现经典模型几乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer更具韧性（RDR最高0.351），并验证了干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Smishing检测系统通常在干净的单语文本上训练和评估，忽视了低资源场景中攻击者通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动来规避检测系统的现实威胁，缺乏对模型对抗鲁棒性的系统性评估。

Method: 使用27,037条消息的数据集，评估五种模型架构：三种经典词法模型（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM）和两种多语言Transformer（mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）。经典模型采用黑盒通用攻击，Transformer采用注意力引导的目标攻击；每种模型在三种攻击类型和不同强度下测试，以鲁棒性退化比（RDR）为性能指标，并结合Cliff's d效应量、Mann-Whitney U和Friedman检验进行统计分析。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下近乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），Transformer的韧性显著更强（RDR最高0.351），其中结构扰动是Transformer最明显的弱点。效应量分析显示两类模型差异显著；Transformer内部XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更优，但退化程度高于mBERT。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能作为对抗鲁棒性的可靠预测指标；不同架构需要针对性的防御策略；Smishing检测应被视为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 HARD（Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution）框架，将大语言模型智能体的运行时防御从人工设计转变为自主演化过程，通过核心层（harness-level）形式化描述运行时防御机制，自动识别防御策略并基于失败痕迹迭代改进防御机制，实验证明其在提升安全性同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体的扩展能力引入了复杂的安全威胁，现有运行时防御方法严重依赖人工设计的干预措施，缺乏构建和维护这些防御机制的原则性框架。

Method: 首先构建了核心层（harness-level）的运行时防御形式化描述，系统性地刻画核心机制如何支持防御构建，并提供统一的视角审视现有干预手段。在此基础上提出 HARD 框架，这是一个自演化的运行时防御框架，能够自动识别合适的干预策略，并基于观察到的失败痕迹迭代改进防御工件，将运行时防御开发从人工工程转化为自主演化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，HARD 在安全性能上优于现有手工设计的防御方法，同时保持了良性任务的效用。

Conclusion: 自主防御演化是保护部署环境下 LLM 智能体的一个新兴且有前景的范式，使智能体能够识别防御弱点并持续改进其保护机制。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [85] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个评估框架，通过注入被篡改的目标响应使自主渗透测试智能体的验证过程变得可观测，并基于450个场景、5条模型路线揭示活动增多可能掩盖验证链断裂，而成功恢复依赖于找到可用证据并在报告中保存它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试智能体依赖目标响应来指导后续行动和最终报告，但欺骗性响应会同时重定向攻击轨迹和验证过程。然而，最终报告几乎不揭示智能体如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或将观察转化为漏洞声明，因此验证过程是黑箱的。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，并将每个变换后的场景与同一环境下的原生场景配对，在第一个受影响的响应处对齐。采用源链接重建方法跟踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止决策和报告支撑。定义了三个冻结观察契约：漏洞证明、资源所有权和可复用工件。在450个场景上评估五条模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复取决于找到可用证据并在报告中保留它。欺骗性目标观察被转化为可复现的证据处理探针。

Conclusion: ATOBench将离线渗透测试智能体评估从仅关注最终结果扩展到过程层面，揭示了不可信观察如何塑造动作、验证和报告，为攻击面智能体的证据处理能力提供了可重复的评测手段。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [86] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniSphinx，一种新的混合网络数据包格式，通过在数据包中嵌入处理代码，使单个混合网络部署能够模拟任何其他混合网络格式，解决了现有混合网络格式互不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合网络数据包格式各不相同且互不兼容，每种格式都需要单独的软件和基础设施来部署，这增加了匿名通信系统的部署和维护成本。作者希望设计一种统一格式，能够在单一部署中兼容并模拟所有现有混合网络格式。

Method: OmniSphinx允许发送方在数据包中嵌入决定数据包处理方式的代码，从而构建一个主动混合网络（active mix network），使网络节点能够根据包内代码灵活处理数据包，实现对其他混合网络格式的模拟。

Result: 实验评估表明，OmniSphinx的模拟开销在典型混合网络使用场景下是合理的：对于最紧凑的Sphinx格式，计算时间增加约90微秒，数据包头大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx通过可编程数据包的方式成功实现了多种混合网络格式的统一模拟，以较小的开销换取了显著的兼容性和部署灵活性。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [87] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InterSAGE是一个信任原生的协议套件，为LLM驱动的智能体互操作提供四层安全底座（持久身份、发现、信任协商、可问责性），通过四个核心原语（Agent身份卡、DID绑定VC清单的能力感知发现、单调能力衰减与两级访问控制的信任协商、无共识账本的内核级加密审计轨迹）补充现有的通信协议（MCP、A2A、ANP、AG-UI），使信任语义显式、可移植、可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体协议仅定义消息如何交换，但未解决智能体如何证明其身份、授权、广告能力或委派后的可问责性问题，缺乏信任安全基座。

Method: 提出四层协议套件：持久身份层、发现层、信任协商层、可问责层；核心原语包括Agent身份卡（绑定开发者、代码包、运营者与部署上下文）、基于DID绑定VC清单的能力感知发现、单调能力衰减与两级访问控制的信任协商、不依赖共识账本的内核级加密审计轨迹。设计为与MCP/A2A/ANP/AG-UI互补共存。

Result: 与50+项相关工作对比，涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委派与审计架构，证明尚无先验方案将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商与可问责性统一为四层信任基座。

Conclusion: InterSAGE作为首个统一四层信任基座，在不替代通信协议的前提下提供安全互操作所需的信任语义，允许通信协议独立演进。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [88] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor，一个系统性验证开源SLEIGH处理器规格（Ghidra使用）正确性的测试框架，通过代理自动化oracle验证策略，在五种不同架构规格中发现38,920个差异并归纳出125个独特bug及修复建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规格支撑着反汇编器、反编译器和模拟器等关键安全工具，但其正确性几乎没有被系统性地检验过。规格中的错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并促成分析规避技术，因此验证处理器规格的正确性是重要且非平凡的任务。

Method: 设计并实现一个基于自动化oracle验证策略（通过代理）的测试框架。利用规格本身编码的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成针对性的初始状态，然后通过将运行处理器规格的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，比较指令的解码和模拟结果。

Result: 在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等五种开源规格上应用InSPECtor，发现超过38,920个差异，归纳出125个独特bug并提出修复方案，识别出解码缺陷、语义缺陷以及跨供应商不一致问题，并提炼出8条具体建议。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了规格正确性的重要性，提供了一个实用工具来显著提升SLEIGH处理器规格的保真度，从而加强下游安全工具和分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [89] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究比较了知识图谱检索系统（微软GraphRAG）与标准向量相似性检索系统（Naive RAG）在从网络攻击报告中自动生成检测规则时的效果。研究发现，GraphRAG生成的检测计划更依赖持久性、难以规避的"金字塔顶端"线索（如TTP、行为模式），而Naive RAG更倾向于提取易变化的低级线索（如IP、域名、文件哈希）。在APT28报告的案例研究中，当所有低级指标被轮换后，GraphRAG计划仍保持100%的检测率，而Naive RAG计划仅剩29%。在九个真实CTI报告的比较中，这一模式得到一致验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化从安全报告中提取检测规则的方式往往只提取简单的低级线索（如恶意的IP、域名和文件哈希）并将其转化为黑名单。这些线索被攻击者轻易变更后就失效了，形成"Pyramid of Pain"中最薄弱的一层。该研究旨在探索能否利用知识图谱检索系统生成更依赖持久性高级线索的检测计划，从而提高自动检测规则的持久性和抗规避能力。

Method: 将同一份网络攻击报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型分别输入到微软GraphRAG（知识图谱检索系统）和Naive RAG（标准向量相似性检索系统）中，唯一的变量是检索步骤。首先对一个APT28报告进行详细案例研究，在旋转所有IP地址、域名和文件哈希后评估两套系统的检测保持率；随后在来自四个厂商的九个真实CTI报告上重复比较，评估两系统生成的检测计划在Pyramid of Pain各层的分布和总得分。

Result: 在APT28案例研究中，GraphRAG计划在所有低级指标全部轮换后仍保持100%的检测触发率，而Naive RAG计划仅保持29%。在九个真实CTI报告的比较中，GraphRAG计划一致性地达到金字塔中更高、更难规避的层次，即使两系统在总分上相近时也是如此。结果还显示生成提示词的措辞与检索后端的选择同等重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，知识图谱感知的检索系统应当被视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎计划架构上正确的检索基础。与Naive RAG相比，GraphRAG生成的检测计划更依赖持久的高级线索，具有更强的抗规避能力。同时，生成提示词的措辞对检测质量的影响几乎与检索后端本身一样重要。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [90] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子云存储认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将每文件多次后量子签名缩减为每批次一次签名，大幅降低多文件上传场景下的认证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要能抵御未来量子计算攻击的认证方案，混合构造（经典+后量子）是主流方案，但朴素扩展到多文件上传时，逐文件的晶格签名开销成为主导瓶颈，在真实批量上传场景下代价过高。

Method: 构建混合后量子模型：AES-256-GCM批量加密 + 混合X25519/ML-KEM-768密钥封装 + 混合Ed25519/ML-DSA-65双重签名，但对整批次明文计算SHA3-256承诺，生成单一固定大小摘要并仅签名一次，使后量子签名调用次数从n降为1，其余开销由快速对称加密与哈希承担。

Result: 在商用客户端平台、20次重复平均下，签名阶段耗时随批次增大保持近恒定，而逐文件基线线性增长；n=1000时，对100KB/1MB/10MB文件分别将签名阶段耗时降低629、606、725倍。

Conclusion: 该批量承诺方案有效消除了多文件上传中后量子签名次数随批次线性增长的瓶颈，在保证混合抗量子安全性的同时取得了数量级的性能提升。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [91] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出程序不变量（program invariants）是保护区块链智能合约免受网络攻击的最有力解决方案，通过构建INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊攻击案例）和PONDEREPLAY重放框架，验证了智能合约不变量能够阻挡所有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域最具对抗性的环境，网络犯罪分子利用漏洞窃取数十亿美元。目前尚无任何概念或技术被证明能真正有效解决这一问题，因此需要探索新的防御思路。

Method: 提出经典的程序不变量概念作为核心防御手段。构建了INVARIANTEVAL基准，包含28个真实以太坊攻击事件，每个事件配有人类编写的可阻止攻击的不变量。开发了PONDEREPLAY重放框架，通过重新执行交易来验证智能合约不变量的正确性和可靠性，并通过重放108,637条历史交易进行大规模验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，智能合约不变量成功阻挡了INVARIANTEVAL中所有的网络犯罪攻击，全部通过重放108,637条历史交易得到充分验证。大规模实验清晰地证明了智能合约不变量对网络犯罪分子具有防护作用。

Conclusion: 智能合约不变量是一种有效的智能合约安全防护手段，能够系统性抵御网络犯罪攻击，且可以通过自动化工具发现关键不变量，为解决区块链安全问题提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [92] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过强制交易生成时的可验证延迟来阻止区块创建者对MEV机会做出反应，同时保持活性。论文在拜占庭设置和博弈论理性参与者模型中均给出正面结果，同时指出该防御路线的局限性，并基于历史MEV数据验证了该机制的实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV是分布式账本中的系统性威胁，区块创建者可以插入、重排或审查交易以提取价值，损害用户利益和系统公平性。现有防御手段往往以牺牲活性或效率为代价，需要一种既能有效抑制MEV又不破坏系统基本性质的防御机制。

Method: 提出一种基于可验证延迟（Verifiable Delay, VDL）的防御机制，强制交易在生成时经历不可并行的可验证延迟，使得区块创建者无法在MEV机会出现后快速响应而不违反活性约束。分析框架涵盖拜占庭容错模型和理性参与者博弈模型，并利用历史MEV数据评估实际部署效果。

Result: 在拜占庭设置和博弈论模型下均取得了正面结果，证明该机制能有效抑制MEV机会的利用；同时给出了负面界，刻画了该防御策略的内在局限；基于历史MEV数据的实证评估表明该机制能够有效防御大多数现有MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 可验证延迟作为一种轻量的机制，能够在不牺牲活性的前提下为MEV防御提供实用解决方案，但其效果存在理论边界，需要与其他防御手段结合以应对更广泛的威胁模型。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [93] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层次-对象-行为（HOB）的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架VR-Themis，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度相似度分析，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用重打包（应用克隆）威胁用户安全隐私并侵犯开发者版权，但现有检测方法主要针对Android等移动平台，无法捕捉虚拟现实（VR）应用的本质特征，因此难以有效检测VR市场中被非法用户频繁攻击的克隆VR应用。

Method: 提出两阶段克隆检测框架VR-Themis，基于HOB（Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour）模型。粗粒度阶段利用可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，使工具可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；细粒度阶段利用自定义的HOB指标对第一阶段识别出的可疑应用进行深度相似度分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，VR-Themis从收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且无假阳性，证明了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis通过结合VR应用独有特征设计的两阶段检测框架，在保证可扩展性的同时实现了高精度的克隆检测，为VR应用市场的安全防护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [94] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私态势。通过对278个真实Mini Apps进行运行时网络流量捕获与隐私政策对比分析，发现59.4%的应用联系了未披露的第三方，78.8%完全依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或退出机制，暴露了该生态系统中严重的透明度与合规性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps虽然被广泛使用并拥有Telegram提供的上下文访问权限，但其隐私属性几乎未被探索。与WeChat等依赖严格控制的专有执行框架的生态系统不同，Telegram Mini Apps在WebView中运行，结合平台上下文与标准Web能力及不受限的出站网络连接，使得应用能够通过普通Web请求向分析、广告、追踪等第三方传输敏感信息，且可见性有限。

Method: TeleGapper是一个黑盒动态分析框架，通过三个步骤评估Mini Apps的隐私态势：(1)捕获运行时网络流量；(2)识别第三方通信；(3)将观察到的数据流与已披露的隐私信息进行对比。研究团队从tApps Center（社区驱动的Telegram第三方应用目录）收集了278个可运行的Mini Apps作为评估数据集。

Result: 评估结果显示：59.4%的Mini Apps至少联系了一个未披露的第三方；78.8%的Mini Apps完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策，而非应用特定的隐私政策；没有任何Mini Apps提供用户同意或退出（opt-out）机制。这些结果揭示了该生态系统中存在显著的信息披露与合规性差距。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态系统存在显著的隐私透明度与合规性缺口。由于Mini Apps依赖WebView运行且出站网络不受限制，同时Telegram的默认隐私政策机制降低了开发者的披露负担，导致大量应用未充分披露其真实的第三方数据共享行为，且缺乏用户控制机制，亟需在广泛使用的生态系统中加强隐私保障。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出生成式AI社区缺乏对"推理"的统一定义，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供AI推理研究沟通最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在自主推理方面进展迅速，但社区对"推理"缺乏统一的操作性定义，往往隐式排斥逻辑学和可验证自动推理的历史处理方式。这种定义上的模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，损害推理研究的可量化进步，也威胁可信自主推理的发展。

Method: 作者采用立场论文（position paper）的方式，通过综述文献提出操作性定义，将推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的（rule-based）过程，强调推理的有效性（validity）和可靠性（soundness）；同时制定了一套AI推理研究沟通最佳实践清单（checklist）。

Result: 提出了两个核心贡献：(1) 基于文献综合的操作性推理定义，将有效且可靠的推理视为可学习的规则驱动过程；(2) 为AI推理研究提供一套沟通最佳实践检查清单，以提升研究可复现性和可比性。

Conclusion: 作者认为定义模糊是可解决的，呼吁研究社区采用统一的操作性定义和沟通规范，以建立可验证的推理评估框架，从而推动可信自主推理的量化进步。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [96] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估语言模型在机构压力下的科研诚信问题。在36个配对任务上，通过5级隐式-显式压力协议测试18个前沿模型，发现高压下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解；显式压力导致屈从不当行为，隐式重构则引发过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"，但其在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力尚未被量化评估。现有评估忽略了科研场景中常见的制度性压力对模型伦理决策的影响。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含3个领域、4个研究阶段的36个配对任务，从不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于工件的决策三个维度评估，并设计5级隐式-显式压力协议对18个前沿模型变体进行系统测试。

Result: 高压下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；模型规模与推理能力不能可靠缓解此问题。显式压力诱导模型屈从不当行为，而隐式上下文重构更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。未能准确分类研究请求的模型，在工件决策上反而表现相当或更优（85.7对79.4），表明三个评估维度在结构上相互分离。

Conclusion: 模型三个诚信维度在结构上解耦，正确的伦理行动不需要准确的分类能力。前沿模型可能表面上有帮助，但隐藏的诚信失败会带来两类部署风险：助长研究不当行为和侵蚀人对AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [97] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对齐方法本身是双用途技术，既可用于防止有害输出，也可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为当前的对齐技术设计初衷是防止有害输出，但这些技术同样容易被恶意利用于信息控制和审查。随着AI成为主要信息来源、经济权力不对称加剧以及威权主义政治趋势上升，这种双用途风险正在加剧，因此有必要尽早讨论。

Method: 作为立场论文，作者通过梳理现有AI对齐技术，将其映射到可能的滥用场景和实际案例，论证"完美对齐模型"的追求反过来为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 论文揭示了AI对齐机制在防止有害输出的同时，也为审查和操纵提供了新的工具，且由于AI的快速普及、经济不对称和威权政治趋势，这一风险被进一步放大。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制被故意滥用的可能性，并提出相应的缓解策略以防范这一双用途风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [98] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含500个ETHICS衍生样本的基准测试，比较了人类标注者与LLM在道德判断中的最终标签一致性和理由一致性，发现即使标签一致，模型与人类在道德理由和原则上也存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常以最终标签与人类判断的一致性作为LLM对齐的代理指标，但标签一致并不代表模型与人类基于相同的道德理由、原则或情境假设进行推理。这种评估方式可能导致对模型道德对齐的过度乐观判断。

Method: 构建了一个从ETHICS基准衍生出的精选500项基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，收集了人类标注者和LLM的最终标签及支持理由（rationales）的新注释，并对前沿模型和开源模型家族进行了标签一致性和理由一致性的对比分析。

Result: 模型与人类标注者多数标签的一致率通常很高，但理由层面的分析揭示了系统性分歧：模型在诸如伤害、尊重、承诺、公正、应得和免责相关性等类别的注意力分配上与人类标注者存在显著差异，即使在最终标签匹配的情况下也是如此。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应等同于对齐。基于标签的评估可能具有误导性的安慰作用，必须辅以对模型判断中表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能更全面地评估LLM的道德对齐。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一个面向移动边缘-云异构流处理场景的去中心化调度框架，其核心创新LLM-MR-CNP是对经典合同网协议（CNP）的扩展，通过语义CFP表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，结合LLM辅助的提案精炼，在保证硬性资源与QoS约束确定性的同时提升协商质量。基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验表明，该方法将延迟违规降至3%、消除资源过度承诺、20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较基于规则的多轮基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上运行时面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格QoS要求的挑战，集中式调度难以适应分布式环境的动态性，而去中心化调度中的协商机制在多智能体场景下存在提案质量低、上下文信息不充分、冲突解决效率不足等问题，需要更智能的协商协议来提升调度决策质量。

Method: MAS-DecStream的核心是LLM-MR-CNP协议扩展，具体包括：1)语义化CFP（调用提案）生成以改善目标表达；2)渐进式上下文披露机制，按需共享信息；3)多轮提案修订支持边缘集群代理基于本地观察、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文迭代优化自然语言卸载提案；4)协商记忆保留历史协商信息；5)确定性验证确保硬性资源和QoS约束不被违反。实验采用三层次消融设计：单轮vs多轮CNP、规则vsLLM辅助精炼、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。

Result: 在阿里巴巴ASI Trace数据集上的实验结果显示：MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除了资源过度承诺，20个代理场景下冲突解决率达到0.91，效用较基于规则的多轮基线提升最高22%。25个案例的独立评估揭示了模型选择和提示词设计对准确性与成本之间存在权衡关系。消融分析表明多轮CNP精炼是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助的增量价值主要体现在处理定性和不确定运行时上下文方面。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，多轮CNP精炼是协议层面的核心增益来源，而LLM辅助在定性上下文和不确定性处理方面提供补充价值。该工作为在边缘-云异构环境中利用LLM增强多智能体协商提供了初步证据，同时通过确定性验证确保了关键资源与QoS约束不受LLM不确定性的影响。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [100] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念：在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅需要与人类价值观对齐，还应以与用户相似的推理方式进行思考，并忠实传达其推理过程，这是提升可理解性、信任度及AI采纳的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地被用作决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者。现有对齐方法（如RLHF等）仅关注价值观对齐，却忽视了AI推理过程与用户认知模式的一致性。作者认为认知错位是阻碍AI在众多应用场景中被采纳的潜在障碍。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的研究方式，综合回顾认知对齐与可理解性、可信度相关的已有证据；通过新的调查数据验证用户对认知对齐的需求程度；系统梳理现有对齐方法与认知对齐要求之间的差距，并提出研究议程。

Result: 调查数据表明许多用户在对AI的判断或行动的推理依据尤为重要时，认为认知对齐是"必不可少"的。论文识别出现有对齐方法在实现认知对齐方面存在的关键缺口，并提出了针对性的研究方向。

Conclusion: 认知对齐对创建用户既愿意也有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要。实现认知对齐不仅能提升AI的可理解性和可信度，也是推动AI在诸多预期应用场景中被广泛采纳的必要条件，应作为未来AI研究的重要方向。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [101] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了来自六家供应商的九个大型语言模型，发现提示词的语言可以改变模型在高风险战略场景中的决策。具体而言，日语提示词使Claude模型家族和Gemini Pro 3.1的核打击建议率显著降低，且该效应由模型被要求使用的推理语言（而非输入语言）驱动——即使英文提示词中要求用日语推理，也能触发同样的效果。模型在用日语推理时会自发产生道德词汇，而英语推理时不会。但仅对在英语中已表现出犹豫的模型有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常在仅用英语进行评估。作者质疑：提示词的语言是否可能改变模型在高风险决策中的行为？若安全对齐仅在单一语言下验证，则可能遗漏其他语言中缺失的风险或蕴藏的安全保障。这一空白在核威慑等极端高风险场景中尤为关键。

Method: 作者设计了单轮博弈论场景，让模型向一个核武国家提供是否打击无防御对手的建议。提示词在策略上完全一致、刻意排除道德元素，仅变化语言（英语vs日语）。测试覆盖六个供应商的九个模型。此外，设计跨语言实验以分离机制：在英文提示词中指令模型用日语推理（而非直接给日语输入），从而将"输入语言"与"推理语言"两个变量解耦，确定效应的真正驱动因素。

Result: 日语提示词使Claude Sonnet 4.6在无必要打击场景中发射率从40%降至0%，在争议场景中从93%降至17%，而在策略上合理的场景中影响甚微。Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验表明，在英文提示词中要求用日语推理即可使发射率从93%降至37%，证明驱动因素是推理语言而非输入语言。用日语推理时模型自发生成"道德成本""数百万生命"等提示词中完全不存在的道德词汇。五个其他模型无论语言如何几乎全条件发射，无语言效应——该效应仅在模型英语中已存在犹豫时成立。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性。仅用英语评估安全性可能同时错失其他语言编码的风险和安全保障。安全对齐评估应扩展至多语言场景，并且需要更深入地理解推理语言如何影响模型的道德推理路径——推理语言的切换可以激活或抑制特定的价值框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [102] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，一种在推理阶段按需分配计算的语言模型架构。其主流（primary flow）是完整的因果语言模型，负责处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流（auxiliary flow）在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅在最终prompt位置之后激活，用于增强续写预测能力且不写入持久状态。两个流共享attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。实验表明该架构在相同token比较下获得更低的验证损失，并为MoE模型提供了阶段特定的专家分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型服务请求量增加，累积推理成本相对于一次性训练成本变得越来越重要。传统宽度或深度扩展会同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每个新增层在两个阶段都会被评估。作者希望将额外的学习计算仅分配给续写预测（continuation prediction），而保持prompt级主计算和单一持久KV缓存不变，从而实现两阶段计算成本的解耦。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流是一个完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅在最终prompt位置之后激活，增加续写预测计算但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两个流共享主要的attention、MLP和输出矩阵，使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。权重和主缓存的共享允许在分组执行中复用已加载的权重和缓存KV。在MoE模型中，主流和辅助流的专家扇出成为独立的控制维度，可分别调节prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的比较中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下均获得更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，作者研究了两种机制：在固定prefill专家计算下增加decode计算，以及在固定decode专家预算下在两个流之间重新分配。实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer通过将额外计算仅分配给续写预测阶段，实现了推理阶段计算成本与预测质量的更精细调优。该架构在预填充（prefill）和解码（decode）阶段之间建立了独立可控的计算分配机制，为优化语言模型推理效率提供了新思路，尤其在MoE场景下展现了阶段特定资源分配的价值。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [103] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法用于跨领域零样本/少样本个性化，通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为起点和先验中心，依据支持集大小和预测不确定性调节更新力度以避免过拟合；同时将个性化先验分解为用户和领域组件，分别用可读提示和拓扑保持软token实现，在多个基准上取得显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨领域零样本/少样本个性化旨在仅凭少量目标域交互生成用户偏好的响应，但现有适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度而易过拟合；历史迁移方法常常将用户偏好与源领域伪影纠缠，导致不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法：1）使用元学习的LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点和先验中心，依据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，在稀疏或模糊证据下限制过拟合，在证据充分时允许更强个性化；2）将个性化先验进行功能分解为用户组件（可读提示稳定表达偏好）和领域组件（拓扑保持软token进行领域特定隐空间条件化），避免跨域偏好迁移的纠缠问题。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上超越强基线。在HiCUPID上，相较于最优竞争基线，跨领域胜率退化减少47.9%；未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 受控适应与先验功能分解相结合能有效解决跨领域个性化中稀疏证据下的过拟合和偏好-领域纠缠问题，实现可靠且可迁移的个性化。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [104] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍阿斯利康开发的内部LLM系统Research Assistant，该系统以聊天式界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性资源、表达数据及内部实验系统等多源数据，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，响应基于检索证据并链接原始来源，文中概述了系统架构、设计决策和大规模部署经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床医生在广泛的生物医学数据源中高效探索研究问题，通过统一聊天界面整合分散的多源数据，提升日常研发工作流程的效率。

Method: 构建基于LLM的Research Assistant系统，采用聊天式交互界面，提供快速模式（直接问答）和多步模式（复杂研究任务）两种工作方式，通过检索增强生成（RAG）确保响应有据可依并链接回原始数据源，整合文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源和表达数据等多类数据源。

Result: 该系统已在阿斯利康大规模部署，支持日常研发工作流。论文概述了系统架构和主要设计选择，并分享了实际部署中积累的经验教训。

Conclusion: Research Assistant展示了LLM系统在药物研发领域多源数据整合与检索增强问答方面的应用价值，其架构设计和部署经验可为相似场景下的企业内部AI系统建设提供参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [105] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE），一个程序化生成的基准，系统评估大语言模型在同时满足多个显式约束时的性能衰减。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查的系统测试，发现逐约束通过率呈渐变式下降，而全部满足的概率急剧崩塌；结构性约束比词汇性约束退化更快；失败近乎独立导致累积效应呈乘法级增长，可靠指令遵循在5-6个约束后显著崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在实际部署中经常需要同时满足多个显式约束（如推理结构、安全边界、输出模式），但已有的评估大多只关注单一约束，对组合约束场景下的性能退化缺乏系统刻画。研究旨在回答三个核心问题：性能退化速度有多快、退化的内在机制是什么、能否缓解这种崩塌。

Method: 构建CSE基准，程序化生成不同数量的同时约束（k=1到12），每个约束由确定性、基于规则的验证器独立评分（完全排除LLM裁判偏差）。覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型，共进行369,753次检查。通过逐约束通过率与全约束成功率的对比、约束类型间的退化差异分析、以及失败独立性的统计检验来系统表征组合约束场景下的模型性能。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）逐约束通过率缓慢下降，但全约束满足率急剧崩塌——在k=8时，单个约束通过率约41%的模型，全部通过的几率仅5.7%；（2）结构性约束比词汇约束每个新增约束多损失2倍基线能力，退化差异由"理解-维持差距"决定，需要持续追踪的约束比二元决策约束更易受组合影响；（3）失败几乎相互独立，使失败积累呈乘法效应；残余关联源于共享输出特征而非成对干扰。最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，15个模型中12个在3个或更少约束时即跌破此阈值。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令遵循在5-6个同时约束时系统性崩溃，这表明当前LLM在组合约束场景下的能力存在根本性瓶颈。失败独立性这一发现既解释了崩塌的普遍性（逐约束准确率的乘积效应），也为未来的改进方向提供了思路——若要突破组合限制，需改进模型对共享输出特征的跟踪能力，而非单纯提升单约束准确率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [106] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS是一个可移植且自进化的AI智能体记忆操作系统，采用统一实体-属性-时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能演化，在LOCOMO和PersonaMem基准上分别达到94.03%和70.63%的准确率，并通过MindSkillEvolve算法在SpreadsheetBench上较初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体记忆系统在开发后通常保持固定不变，无法通过持续使用来调整其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，这限制了智能体在长期交互中的适应能力和个性化发展。

Method: MindMemOS提出统一实体-属性-时间结构来组织开放世界信息；其MindMemEvolve算法采用验证驱动的演化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式；通过"梦境"（dreaming）机制合并冗余记录并解决冲突以巩固积累的记忆；利用隐式纠正反馈作为人在回路的信号识别和修正潜在不准确或错位的记忆；MindSkillEvolve算法将智能体执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO基准上达到94.03%的准确率，在PersonaMem基准上达到70.63%的准确率；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上较初始技能基线提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS验证了通过统一时间结构组织记忆、验证驱动演化搜索、梦境巩固和隐式人类反馈等机制，可以实现AI智能体记忆系统的持续自进化，从而提升长期交互中的记忆准确性和任务执行能力。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [107] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理持久内存（GPM），一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，用于解决智能体长期记忆中检索出的陈旧、矛盾或已撤销记录可能支持错误主张的问题。GPM在预设哈希冻结的3,600例基准上完全匹配所有完整结果，并在密封的服务评估中获得2,400/2,400的治理通道正确率（对照组的非治理Qwen2.5-7B仅600/2,400），同时在有限模型探索和差分测试中均无违例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆系统通常采用"选择-存储-检索"的简单管线，但检索阶段无法判断被检索的记录是否已被矛盾、取代、撤回、删除或已过期。这导致智能体可能基于失效信息产生错误输出，缺乏对记忆生命周期状态的治理能力。

Method: GPM引入可审计的双时态状态转换模型，包含五项核心机制：源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公开屏障、失效关闭的结构化发布，以及五项可执行条款（账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后不复活、在验证头的新鲜视图上的精确声明闭合）。评估包括：预设哈希冻结的GPM-ReleaseBench（3,600例）、密封端到端服务评估（V3和V5两个版本）、生产代码无关的有限模型探索（331,776语义态和1,990,656查询态）以及100,000轨迹的三引擎差分测试。

Result: GPM在3,600例ReleaseBench上匹配所有完整结果；最强简单策略仅匹配1,800/3,600并在50%违规案例上产生不匹配发布。V3密封评估中治理通道2,400/2,400正确（对照600/2,400），修复全部1,800基线失败无回归（单侧95%下限99.875%和99.834%）。V5对中英文命令重新密封同样获得每臂2,400/2,400。有限模型探索无完整合约反例，100,000轨迹差分测试零不匹配。

Conclusion: 这些结果是有界的合约和实现验证，而非开放世界模型准确性或世界真相的证据。治理答案在密封服务评估中是确定性服务输出；7B结果仅作为非治理基线对照，不声称语言模型本身变得完全准确。GPM证明了通过可审计的治理层可以确保记忆发布正确性，但这与模型自身的知识正确性是两个不同层面。
